Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [194] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM框架适配到语音扩散模型领域，提出交叉注意力归因方法，分析CapSpeech-TTS中自然语言风格描述词如何影响声学输出。通过对3,600组(风格描述词,文本转录)组合的分析，揭示了风格标记在跨注意力层中的时空分布特性：全局性调节、与F0/能量的相关性、早步深层的激活峰值，以及第17层注意力熵最小与风格重要性峰值共现的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述文本到语音合成系统虽能通过自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词语如何影响声学输出的机制尚不明确，这阻碍了表达性TTS中故障模式的诊断和可控性的提升。

Method: 将DAAM（扩散注意力属性映射）框架首次适配到语音领域，提出针对语音扩散模型的交叉注意力归因方法。对CapSpeech-TTS提取跨25层和24个ODE步骤的逐词注意力热图，分析120个风格描述词×30个文本转录共3,600组组合。

Result: (1) 风格标记的时间方差低于内容/功能标记，验证了全局调节特性；(2) 风格注意力与基频(F0)和能量相关；(3) 风格调节在早期ODE步骤和深层网络中达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明网络在最关键风格阶段具有最大选择性。

Conclusion: 本研究首次揭示了自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中的交叉注意力机制，为理解和改进基于文本风格控制的TTS系统提供了新的分析工具和理论基础。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [195] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型（MoE）在分布偏移下的校准行为，证明硬路由模型中专家级校准足以保证整体模型校准，但软路由模型则不足，并提出了对抗性重加权方法以改善校准-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作表明在个体预测器层面强制校准可提升集成模型和MoE的准确率与校准性能，但校准在何种条件下有助于MoE尚不明确。本文旨在理解MoE模型在分布偏移下的行为，特别是路由机制与专家级校准之间的相互作用。

Method: 1) 理论分析硬路由和软路由MoE模型在分布偏移下的校准性质；2) 提出对抗性重加权方法，惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差。

Result: 硬路由MoE中专家级校准足以保证整体模型在广泛分布偏移下的校准性；软路由MoE中专家级校准不足以保证整体校准；提出的对抗性重加权方法改善了平均和困难子集上的准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 对抗性重加权方法能有效提升MoE模型在分布偏移下的校准性能，在不同模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移场景下均表现出改善效果。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [The Representational Limit of Scalar Interactions: An Interventional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19410)
*Potito Aghilar,Sabino Roccotelli,Stanislao Fidanza,Vito Walter Anelli,Sebastiano Stramaglia,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Stochastic Hi-Fi方法，通过干预式掩码推理分解每个特征在预测中的独特性(U)、冗余性(R)和协同性(S)，解决了符号化成对交互分数混淆这三种机制的根本问题。在合成SCM、GPT-2 IOI电路和NIH ChestX-ray14数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成对交互指标（如Shapley-Taylor、Shapley Interaction）从根本上混淆了特征的独特性(U)、冗余性(R)和协同性(S)三种机制，无法准确分解预测中的特征贡献。

Method: 提出Stochastic Hi-Fi，一种无需重训练的后验可预测性分解方法。通过干预式掩码推理估计每个特征的U/R/S分布，采用耦合菱形采样实现严格方差缩减，并具备有限样本蒙特卡洛界和均匀有限词汇收敛性保证。

Result: 在表格SCM中，相较于标量基线，交互幅度恢复比提升高达411倍；在GPT-2 IOI电路中成功分离冗余头和协同头；在NIH ChestX-ray14上，Pointing Game匹配GradCAM表现，Deletion AUC显著优于GradCAM。

Conclusion: Stochastic Hi-Fi能够准确解耦特征交互中的独特性、冗余性和协同性，恢复被传统标量指标掩盖的结构信息，在可解释性方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: Signed pairwise interaction scores fundamentally conflate uniqueness (U), redundancy (R), and synergy (S). We prove this on a minimal 3-way XOR structural causal model: faithful indices such as Shapley-Taylor return zero per pair, whereas projective indices such as Shapley Interaction spread the third-order effect into pair scalars that conflate the three mechanisms. We introduce Stochastic Hi-Fi, a post-hoc, retraining-free predictability decomposition that estimates per-feature U/R/S profiles by interventional masked inference. The estimator provides exact interventional semantics, finite-sample Monte Carlo bounds, strict variance reduction from coupled diamond sampling, and uniform finite-vocabulary convergence. Across tabular SCMs, Stochastic Hi-Fi recovers structure missed by scalar baselines (up to 411x larger interaction-magnitude recovery ratios). It also separates redundant and synergistic heads in the GPT-2 IOI circuit. On NIH ChestX-ray14, Stochastic Hi-Fi matches GradCAM on Pointing Game and improves substantially on Deletion AUC.

</details>


### [197] [A Solver-Free Training Method for Predict-then-Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19587)
*Beichen Wan,Mo Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于测度变换原理的决策导向学习框架，在训练过程中完全无需调用优化求解器，通过新的替代损失函数实现可扩展的预测-优化训练，在保持决策质量的同时将训练时间缩短数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预测-优化范式中，直接最小化经验决策遗憾对于线性规划和组合优化问题不可行，因为决策映射是分段常数且梯度几乎处处为零。现有方法通过平滑微分过程来解决，但每次梯度评估都需要调用计算昂贵的求解器，存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 基于测度变换原理提出决策导向学习框架，构建完全无需优化求解器的替代损失函数，避免了训练过程中对求解器的重复调用。

Result: 经验上，该方法在决策质量上与最先进方法相当，同时将训练时间减少了数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法通过消除训练中对优化求解器的依赖，显著提升了预测-优化范式的可扩展性，并具备Fisher一致性等理论保证。

Abstract: We propose a scalable method for training prediction (machine learning) models in the predict-then-optimize paradigm, where model outputs serve as coefficients for a subsequent linear optimization task. Directly minimizing the empirical decision regret is intractable for linear programming and combinatorial optimization since the decision mapping is piecewise constant, and the gradients are zero almost everywhere. While existing methods address this by smoothing the differentiation process, they suffer from scalability issues, since a computationally expensive solver call is required for every gradient evaluation. To address this, we propose a decision-focused learning pipeline based on a measure transformation principle, which yields a new surrogate loss that is completely optimization-solver-free during training. We establish theoretical guarantees, including Fisher consistency and excess risk bounds. Empirically, our method achieves decision quality competitive with state-of-the-art methods while reducing training time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [198] [Variational Consensus Monte Carlo for Bayesian Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19643)
*Julie Fendler,Francesca L. Crowe,Tom Marshall,Sylvia Richardson,Paul D. W. Kirk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在联邦学习环境下对贝叶斯混合模型进行推断的完整流程，采用Consensus Monte Carlo方法，将变分CMC扩展至过拟合贝叶斯混合模型以自动推断聚类数量，并设计了适用于跨数据孤岛的聚类匹配算法和多种聚合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康数据具有隐私性、敏感性和共享限制，现有方法在处理混合模型时假设聚类数量和关键参数已知，缺乏在联邦学习场景下对贝叶斯混合模型进行完整推断的能力。

Method: (i) 将变分CMC扩展至过拟合贝叶斯混合模型，无需共轭性即可推断聚类数量；(ii) 提出适用于跨数据孤岛场景的新型聚类匹配算法；(iii) 针对不同联邦学习约束设计多种聚合推断策略；(iv) 提供实践选择指南。

Result: 模拟研究验证了框架有效性，在局部数据组成反映底层聚类结构时，该方法在恢复小聚类方面比标准MCMC更准确；并在英国老年人群电子健康记录数据中成功识别了多发病模式。

Conclusion: 该方法在联邦学习约束下有效推断混合模型，尤其擅长恢复局部数据反映聚类结构时的小聚类，优于标准池化数据的MCMC方法。

Abstract: Motivated by the privacy, sensitivity and sharing limitations of health data, we present a comprehensive pipeline for inference of Bayesian mixture models within a federated learning setting, i.e. when data cannot be fully shared or pooled across compute nodes. We adopt a Consensus Monte Carlo (CMC) approach, in which an MCMC algorithm is run independently within each data silo to estimate local posterior distributions, which are then aggregated to approximate the posterior over the full data. The variational CMC approach of Rabinovich, Angelino and Jordan (2015) [1] frames the aggregation step as a variational inference problem, but their application to mixtures assumes the number of clusters and key mixture parameters to be known. Our main methodological contributions are: (i) an extension of variational CMC to over-fitted Bayesian mixture models that infer the number of clusters and all model parameters, without requiring conjugacy; (ii) novel cluster-matching algorithms suitable for cross-silo settings in which not every cluster appears in each local dataset; (iii) a number of inference strategies for the aggregation step, matched to different federated learning constraints; and (iv) guidelines for choosing among these in practice. A comprehensive simulation study validates the framework and allows us to compare to state-of-the-art federated learning alternatives. Notably, we show that when the composition of local datasets reflects the underlying clustering structure in the data, our approach can recover small clusters with greater accuracy than standard MCMC applied to the pooled data. We illustrate the framework on large-scale electronic health record data, identifying multi-morbidity patterns in a British geriatric population.

</details>


### [199] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-aware Refinement for LLM-as-a-Judge Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19714)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Weiyi He,Junxi Zhang,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AURA是一个自适应不确定性感知的细化框架，用于在有限人工验证下审计LLM作为评判者的成对比较决策，通过迭代学习人类一致性信号并传播可靠证据来优化信任估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评判审计管线通常假设存在可靠子集或干净监督信号（如人工标注、启发式过滤或强评判者输出），但这一假设在LLM评估中很脆弱：初始划分可能继承评判者偏差，而人工验证通常过于稀缺，无法在规模上定义稳定分组。

Method: AURA框架：（1）迭代学习人类一致性信号；（2）传播可靠证据；（3）优先选择不确定性高的比较进行人工审查。核心思想是将对评判者的信任视为一个随证据积累逐步细化的潜在量。

Result: 在合成数据和真实成对LLM回答数据上进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: AURA通过自适应不确定性感知的细化方法，在有限人工验证条件下有效提升了LLM-as-a-judge的审计可靠性，解决了传统方法中初始偏差和人工稀缺的瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for open-ended generation, as large-scale human evaluation is often expensive and difficult to scale, yet their preferences remain imperfect proxies for human judgment. Existing auditing pipelines often assume that a reliable subset of examples or clean supervision signals are available beforehand, for example from human annotation, heuristic filtering, or the outputs of strong judges. In LLM evaluation, this assumption is fragile: the initial split may inherit judge bias, while human verification is typically too scarce to define stable groups at scale. We propose AURA, an adaptive uncertainty--aware refinement framework for auditing pairwise LLM--as--a--judge decisions under selected human verification. AURA iteratively learns a human-consistency signal, propagates reliable evidence, and prioritizes uncertain comparisons for human review. The key idea is to treat trust in a judge as a latent quantity that is progressively refined as evidence accumulates. We provide a compact formulation, a stable refinement procedure, and a comprehensive evaluation on both synthetic and real pairwise LLM-answer data.

</details>


### [200] [Stochastic Linear Contextual Bandits with Bounded Noise: A Set-Membership Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20022)
*Haonan Xu,Yingying Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对带界奖励噪声的随机线性上下文赌博机问题，提出SME-OFU算法，利用集合成员估计和乐观面对不确定性原则，实现了O(log T)的遗憾界，优于传统次高斯噪声假设下的Õ(√T)界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机线性上下文赌博机工作通常假设次高斯奖励噪声和有界期望奖励，得到Õ(√T)的最优遗憾界。然而实际应用中观测/实现奖励自然有界，这意味着奖励噪声有界。有界噪声比次高斯条件包含更多信息，但在现有文献中尚未被显式利用。

Method: 提出SME-OFU算法，采用集合成员估计（SME）进行不确定性量化，并结合乐观面对不确定性（OFU）原则。

Result: 在带界奖励噪声条件下，SME-OFU算法实现了O(log T)的改进遗憾界，这并不与次高斯噪声下Õ(√T)的最优界矛盾，因为有界噪声是更强的条件。仿真实验表明，当奖励噪声有界时，SME-OFU相比为次高斯噪声设计的基准算法具有经验上的提升。

Conclusion: 通过利用有界噪声这一更强但更符合实际的条件，SME-OFU算法显著降低了遗憾界从Õ(√T)到O(log T)，并在仿真中验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper considers stochastic linear contextual bandits (SLCB) with bounded reward noise. Existing works typically assume sub-Gaussian reward noise and bounded expected rewards, under which the optimal regret bound scales as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ in terms of horizon $T$. However, in many applications, realized/observed rewards are also naturally bounded, implying bounded reward noise. Bounded noise is more informative than the sub-Gaussian condition but has not been leveraged explicitly in the SLCB literature. In this paper, we propose a novel algorithm SME-OFU by utilizing an uncertainty quantification method called set-membership estimation (SME) and applying the principle of optimism in the face of uncertainty (OFU). Our algorithm enjoys an improved regret bound $O(\log T)$. Notice that this does not contradict the existing optimal bound $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ for sub-Gaussian noise because bounded noise is a stronger condition. Finally, simulations show empirical improvements of SME-OFU over a benchmark algorithm designed for sub-Gaussian noise when the reward noise is bounded.

</details>


### [201] [AK-MCS-C2 : Active Kriging Monte Carlo Simulation method with conformal certification for failure probability estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20191)
*Edgar Jaber,Vincent Chabridon,Mathilde Mougeot*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合主动克里金蒙特卡洛模拟与共形预测的主动学习框架，用于结构可靠性分析中的失效概率估计。该方法采用自适应交叉共形策略和J+GP共形估计器，在小样本设置下提供分布无关的预测误差保证，提高了失效概率估计的准确性和鲁棒性，特别是针对稀有事件场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AK-MCS方法在失效概率估计中缺乏对预测误差的可靠保证，尤其在样本稀缺和稀有事件场景下，分类精度和不确定性量化能力不足。

Method: 将共形预测集成到主动克里金蒙特卡洛模拟框架中，采用自适应交叉共形策略，并配合J+GP共形估计器，在小样本和克里金代理模型条件下提供分布无关的预测误差保证。

Result: 可复现的数值实验表明该方法在标准基准测试上优于经典方法，在失效概率估计的准确性和鲁棒性方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过共形预测提供的分布无关保证，显著改善了极限状态面附近样本的分类可靠性，尤其对稀有事件失效概率估计具有重要价值。

Abstract: We introduce a novel active-learning framework for failure probability estimation in structural reliability analysis that integrates Active Kriging Monte Carlo simulation with conformal prediction. The proposed approach employs an adaptive cross-conformal strategy specifically designed for small-sample settings and kriging surrogate models using the J+GP conformal estimator. Unlike standard AK-MCS methods, the proposed framework provides distribution-free guarantees on prediction errors, leading to more reliable classification of samples near the limit-state surface. This improved uncertainty quantification enhances both the accuracy and robustness of failure probability estimates, especially for rare-event regimes where such efficiency is crucial. Reproducible numerical results illustrate the effectiveness of the method and also compare it to classical approaches on well-established benchmarks.

</details>


### [202] [Off-Policy Evaluation for Missingness-Aware Policies in MDPs with Rewards Missing Not at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20206)
*Ziheng Wei,Annie Qu,Rui Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对离线强化学习中奖励因稀疏记录或右删失而缺失（且缺失非随机，MNAR）的问题，提出了一种基于阴影变量和桥函数的离策略评估（OPE）方法，通过min-max估计恢复条件均值奖励，并建立FQE风格的估计器，在理论和实验上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际离线强化学习场景中（如医疗、营销），日志数据的即时奖励常因记录稀疏、不规则或右删失而缺失，且缺失机制非随机（MNAR），导致标准OPE方法存在选择偏差，亟需一种能在MNAR下进行有效离策略评估的方法。

Method: 形式化奖励依赖的倾向模型，利用未来状态作为阴影变量识别完整数据条件均值奖励；引入桥函数恢复条件均值奖励而不显式建模MNAR机制；通过min-max过程估计桥函数以避免双重采样；提出FQE风格的OPE估计器，允许目标策略依赖历史缺失指示变量。

Result: 建立了所提OPE估计量的一致性和有限样本误差界；在模拟数据和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据集上，所提方法显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能有效处理离线强化学习中奖励MNAR缺失问题，为实际应用场景下的可靠离策略评估提供了理论保证和实用工具。

Abstract: In offline Reinforcement Learning, immediate rewards in logged batch data are often unobserved due to sparse or irregular record-keeping, or censored beyond certain reward values. This issue arises in practical settings, including health care and marketing. We investigate off-policy evaluation (OPE) in finite-horizon Markov decision processes when rewards are missing not at random (MNAR), which breaks ignorability and induces selection bias even after conditioning on states and actions. To address this, we formalize a reward-dependent propensity model and use future states as shadow variables to identify the full-data conditional mean reward. We further introduce a bridge function that recovers the conditional mean reward without explicitly modeling the MNAR mechanism, and estimate it via a min-max procedure to avoid double sampling. Building upon these identification results, we propose an Fitted-Q-Evaluation-style estimator that propagates the recovered rewards while allowing target policies to depend on past missingness indicators. Finally, we establish consistency and finite-sample error bounds for our OPE estimator, and show through experiments the strong performance of our method compared to existing methods on simulated and MIMIC-III Sepsis data.

</details>


### [203] [Statistical Properties of Training & Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20299)
*Itay Lavie,Noam Levi,Yonatan Kahn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从物理学视角审视深度学习，探讨其超越古典统计学的关键特征，重点分析神经缩放定律及其在物理问题中与约束条件和归纳偏置的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的成功违背了经典统计学中许多直觉，但其内在机理仍未被充分理解。作者希望从物理学视角揭示深度学习的关键特征与令人惊讶的表现，并为模型构建中的诸多选择提供合理依据。

Method: 采用物理学视角的分析方法，系统审视深度学习的核心特征，特别关注神经缩放定律，并探讨这些定律与物理应用中的约束条件和归纳偏置之间的相互作用。

Result: 揭示了神经缩放定律等深度学习现象，阐明了这些现象与物理问题中固有约束和偏置的互动关系，为深度学习在物理学领域的应用提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 物理学视角有助于理解深度学习超越经典统计学的根本原因，神经缩放定律等特性在物理问题中需结合具体约束与偏置加以考量，这为构建更有效的物理深度学习模型提供了指导。

Abstract: Deep learning has managed to evade numerous intuitions from classical statistics to achieve unprecedented performance on a number of real-world tasks. In this article, we investigate the key features and surprises of deep learning from a physics-informed perspective, taking care to point out and justify where possible the many choices inherent in constructing a deep learning model. In particular, we review the phenomenon of neural scaling laws and discuss their interplay with the constraints and inductive biases which may be present when applying machine learning to problems in physics.

</details>


### [204] [SSH-Net: A Deep Neural Network for Predicting Failure Time Distribution Functions under Competing Risks with Application to GPU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20451)
*Jie Min,Yueyao Wang,Mengkun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化分段风险深度神经网络（SSH-Net），用于在竞争风险框架下预测工程系统的故障时间。该方法将神经网络结构与数据结构相关联，允许不同协变量组通过独立的子网络影响故障预测，并通过仿真和真实GPU故障数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程领域常面临竞争风险问题，现有深度神经网络在处理此类问题时存在两个挑战：一是网络结构复杂导致超参数调优困难；二是工程系统具有多层级物理结构时，将所有层级视为单一输入组会丢失关键信息。

Method: SSH-Net将神经网络结构与数据结构关联，允许不同协变量组通过分离的子网络分别影响故障预测。网络基于原因特定竞争风险模型构建，输出原因特定风险函数，并采用惩罚对数似然作为损失函数。

Result: 通过仿真研究，使用Brier分数、AUC和预测的原因特定累积发生率函数的RMSE评估预测精度。进一步使用Titan GPU故障时间数据验证了模型预测故障时间分布函数的能力。

Conclusion: SSH-Net通过结构化分段设计有效解决了多层级工程系统中竞争风险预测的超参数调优和信息捕获问题，在仿真和真实数据上均表现出良好的预测性能。

Abstract: Competing risks are commonly observed in engineering fields and can bring challenges to time-to-event data modeling when the application scenarios are complicated. Recently, deep neural networks have received great attention for prediction with competing risks, due to their flexibility and high learning capability. However, the complexity of neural network structure brings extra difficulty in hyperparameter tuning based on different data inputs. Additionally, when an engineered system has complex physical structures with multiple hierarchical levels, treating all structural levels as a single group of inputs may fail to capture critical information. To address the issues, we propose a Structured Segmented Hazard Deep Neural Network (SSH-Net) for failure time prediction under cause-specific competing risks framework. Our approach associates neural network structure with data structures, and allows different covariate groups to impact the failure prediction through separate sub-networks. The neural network is constructed based on a cause-specific competing risks model. The SSH-Net outputs cause-specific hazard functions, and utilizes the penalized log-likelihood as the loss function. The prediction accuracy of SSH-Net is validated through simulation studies by evaluating the Brier score, the area under receiver operating characteristic curves (AUC), and the root mean square error (RMSE) of the predicted cause-specific cumulative incident function. We further demonstrate the model's ability to predict failure time distribution functions using the Titan GPU failure time data.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [A land of monotone plenty, bis repetita: from classical to weak optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19516)
*Virginie Ehrlacher,Rodrigue Lelotte,Luca Nenna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明经典最优传输中的c-循环单调性本质上是零阶最优性条件，并将其推广到弱最优传输问题中作为一阶最优性条件，从而统一和深化了对这一单调性概念的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视c-循环单调性的数学本质，将其从几何直觉的表述转化为最优性条件的语言，并推广到更一般的弱最优传输框架中。

Method: 通过将最优性等价于线性传输成本泛函在径向扰动锥上的非负性，建立c-循环单调性与最优性条件之间的联系；然后将这一视角扩展到弱最优传输问题，利用弱传输成本泛函线性化的一阶条件来定义相应的单调性。

Result: 在经典情形下恢复了文献中的已知结论但给出了重新审视的证明；在弱最优传输情形下建立了对应的单调性刻画；证明该性质在适当假设下刻画了最优传输计划。

Conclusion: c-循环单调性可被统一理解为最优传输问题的（零阶或一阶）最优性条件，这一视角为理解和推广该概念提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The celebrated c-cyclical monotonicity property is shown to boil down to the zeroth-order optimality condition for the optimal transport problem. More precisely, we show that optimality is equivalent to the non-negativity of the linear transport cost functional on the radial cone of admissible perturbations. We then utilise this point of view to extend the c-cyclical monotonicity property to the weak optimal transport problem, for which it corresponds to the first-order optimality condition, namely to the non-negativity of the linearisation of the weak transport cost functional near the optimiser. Altogether, this sheds new light on this monotonicity concept. For both classical and weak optimal transport, we show that this property characterises (under suitable assumptions) optimal transport plans. In the classical case, we recover known results of the literature but with revisited proofs.

</details>


### [206] [On the Limits of Biased Derivative Information for Nonconvex Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19553)
*Anant Shyam,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非凸优化中寻找δ-平稳点的问题，其中梯度 oracle 不仅具有随机性还存在偏差。针对一阶方法，本文给出了紧的下界；针对高阶方法，建立了依赖于偏差的下界，并开发了信任域方法与之匹配；在高偏差设置下，通过高阶方差缩减方案进一步提高了 oracle 复杂度，展示了高阶导数信息在特定情况下的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸优化研究主要关注无偏随机梯度场景，但实际应用中梯度 oracle 往往存在偏差（如函数近似、压缩梯度等）。本文旨在刻画有偏随机梯度下寻找平稳点的最优复杂度，并探索高阶导数信息能否带来收益。

Method: 1) 一阶设置：给出紧的下界，匹配 Ajalloeian 和 Stich (2020) 的上界；2) 高阶设置：建立依赖偏差的下界，开发信任域方法匹配下界；3) 提出高阶方差缩减方案处理高偏差场景。

Result: 一阶方法寻找 O((ε+B²)^{1/2})-平稳点具有紧的下界；高阶方法中，信任域方法在特定偏差范围内匹配下界；高阶方差缩减在高偏差下提升了 oracle 复杂度，在某些情况下高阶信息带来了传统无偏随机设置中无法实现的改进。

Conclusion: 有偏随机梯度下，一阶方法的复杂度下界是紧的；高阶信息在偏差存在时能带来收益，而在无偏随机设置中这种收益已被证明无法实现。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding $δ$-stationary points for $δ> 0$, i.e., $x \in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $||\nabla F(x)|| \le δ$, for smooth, non-convex objectives, where the derivative oracles are not only stochastic but also biased. In the first-order setting, we provide tight lower bounds for finding an $O((ε+ B^2)^{1/2})$-stationary point, for $ε> 0$ and where $B$ is a bound on the gradient bias, matching the upper bounds of Ajalloeian and Stich (2020). We then establish bias-dependent lower bounds for algorithms that use higher-order derivative information for finding $O(ε+ B_{\max})$-stationary points, where $B_{\max}$ is a bound on the maximum bias for all derivatives. To complement these lower bounds, we develop trust-region based methods that, for certain ranges of bias, provide guarantees that match the corresponding lower bounds. We further improve upon the oracle complexity in high bias settings through a higher-order variance reduction scheme, in particular demonstrating the benefits, in some cases, of using higher-order derivative information, whereas such improvements are known to be unattainable for stochastic unbiased settings.

</details>


### [207] [Mean-Field Control with a Common Hidden State under Decentralized Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19639)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ali D. Kara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多决策者系统的分布式最优控制问题，其中决策者共享一个隐藏状态并通过相同信道获取去中心化观测。在无限智能体极限下，问题简化为单一智能体控制问题，并通过确定性测度值控制问题与动态规划递归求解。研究表明：随机化控制动作对最优性是必要的，但混合策略随机化非必需；针对无限群体设计的最优对称策略对有限群体是近似最优的，收敛速率为 $O(1/\sqrt{N})$，且随策略记忆长度呈指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多决策者在共享隐藏状态、去中心化观测条件下的最优控制问题，旨在处理智能体行为仅通过经验分布影响系统动力学和成本的场景，并解决有限与无限群体之间的近似最优性及收敛率问题。

Method: 将无限智能体极限问题转化为单一智能体测度值控制问题，通过策略空间上的动态规划递归求解；利用随机化控制动作的必要性分析及对称策略的近似最优性证明，建立有限群体下的收敛率（$1/\sqrt{N}$ 衰减，与记忆长度指数相关）。

Result: 最优解需要随机化控制动作但不需要混合策略随机化；无限群体最优对称策略对有限群体近似最优，收敛率为 $O(1/\sqrt{N})$，且随记忆长度指数增长。

Conclusion: 本文为具有去中心化观测和共享隐藏状态的多智能体控制问题提供了理论框架，揭示了随机化控制动作的必要性，并量化了有限智能体与极限问题之间的近似误差随群体规模和记忆长度的变化规律。

Abstract: We study optimal control of a system with multiple decision makers who share a common hidden state and receive fully decentralized observations through identical channels. The dynamics of the hidden state and the cost incurred by the agents depend on the agents' actions only through their empirical distribution. In the limit problem with infinitely many agents, the problem reduces to a single agent control problem where the agent affects the hidden state dynamics via the conditional law of the actions given the past values of the hidden state process. We formulate this problem as a deterministic measure valued control problem over the space of policies and provide a dynamic programming recursion.
  We first show that for the limiting problem randomization over the control actions is necessary for optimality. However, randomization over the selection of policies (i.e., mixture policies) is not required. We then show that the optimal symmetric policies designed for the infinite population problem are near optimal for the finite population problem.
  In particular, we establish convergence rates that decay with number of agents as $\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}$, and grow exponentially with the memory length used in the policy.

</details>


### [208] [Counterexample to a conjecture on the pairwise independent correlation gap using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19663)
*Arjun Ramachandra,Karthik Natarajan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 借助AI工具GPT5.5 Pro，给出了Ramachandra和Natarajan（2025）关于成对独立相关间隙猜想的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Ramachandra和Natarajan（2025）提出的成对独立相关间隙猜想。

Method: 使用AI工具GPT5.5 Pro构造反例。

Result: 成功构造出反例，否定了原猜想。

Conclusion: Ramachandra和Natarajan（2025）的猜想不成立。

Abstract: Aided by the AI tool GPT5.5 Pro, we provide a counterexample to a conjecture made by Ramachandra and Natarajan (2025) [Pairwise independent correlation gap, Operations Research Letters, 107255, 6040].

</details>


### [209] [Learning Neural Maximal Lyapunov Functions on $\mathsf{SO}(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19669)
*Adeel Akhtar,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov框架，用于在旋转群SO(n)上学习最大Lyapunov函数，通过Zubov型特征刻画吸引域，并推导了对数映射导数的显式公式以实现高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Lie群上的动力系统建立稳定性保证是一个根本性挑战，因为经典Lyapunov方法无法直接从欧氏空间迁移到弯曲几何上。

Method: 提出基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov架构并证明其逼近能力；利用Zubov型特征刻画最大吸引域；推导对数映射导数的显式可计算公式；设计两阶段训练算法平衡计算效率与精度。

Result: 在低维非线性系统上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为SO(n)上的动力系统学习最大Lyapunov函数，并有效刻画吸引域。

Abstract: Establishing stability guarantees for dynamical systems on Lie groups is a fundamental challenge, as classical Lyapunov methods developed for Euclidean spaces do not directly transfer to curved geometries. In this paper, we propose a framework for learning maximal Lyapunov functions for systems evolving on the special orthogonal group $\mathsf{SO}(n)$. Theoretically, we introduce a neural Lyapunov architecture based on the logarithmic map with proven approximation capabilities, and we formulate the learning problem via a Zubov-type characterization of the maximal region of attraction. A key technical contribution is the derivation of explicit, numerically tractable formulas for the derivative of the logarithmic map, enabling training through a two-phase algorithm that balances computational efficiency and accuracy. Empirically, we validate the approach on a low-dimensional nonlinear system.

</details>


### [210] [Stochastic Representations of Stationary HJBI-Type Variational Inequalities with Bilateral Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19705)
*Sheng Huang,Qingmeng Wei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带双边约束的平稳HJBI型变分不等式的概率表示，提出了两种互补的随机表示：一是通过增广无限时域双人零和随机微分博弈(SDG)，二是通过双人零和混合控制-停止SDG，并证明相应值函数是双边HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为平稳HJBI型双边变分不等式建立概率表示，提供两种互补的随机框架来刻画其解。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 通过扩展控制空间引入两个额外停止符号，将障碍项纳入运行收益，利用无限时域随机递归微分博弈框架；(2) 构建混合控制-停止SDG，引入依赖于对手控制过程的非预期停止策略，基于惩罚无限时域SDG、动态规划方法和向后半群稳定性估计进行证明。

Result: 得到了两种随机表示：增广无限时域SDG的下、上值函数是相应HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解；混合控制-停止博弈的值函数与双边HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解一致。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了双边HJBI变分不等式的两种概率表示，为处理此类问题提供了新的随机分析工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study probabilistic representations for stationary HJBI-type variational inequalities with bilateral constraints. We provide two complementary stochastic representations.The first representation is obtained through an augmented infinite-horizon two-player zero-sum stochastic differential game (SDG). By enlarging the control spaces with two additional stopping symbols, the obstacle terms are incorporated into the running payoff. Using the framework of infinite-horizon stochastic recursive differential games, we show that the resulting lower and upper value functions are the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the corresponding HJBI variational inequalities. The second representation is given by a two-player zero-sum mixed control--stopping SDG. In this formulation, each player chooses both a continuous control and a stopping decision, and the payoff is defined by a BSDE with a random terminal time. To make the stopping component compatible with the Elliott--Kalton strategy framework, we introduce nonanticipative stopping strategies depending on the opponent's control process. The proof is based on penalized infinite-horizon SDGs coupled with their own value functions, together with dynamic programming arguments and stability estimates for backward semigroups. We prove that the value functions of the mixed control--stopping game coincide with the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the bilateral HJBI variational inequalities.

</details>


### [211] [Signature Methods for Optimal Market Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19772)
*Alberto Gennaro,Thibaut Mastrolia,Francesca Primavera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于签名的方法来解决均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题，通过签名线性化技术将其简化为对增强市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化，并开发了Sig-REINFORCE算法学习最优买卖报价，在两种场景（泊松过程和自激霍克斯过程）下进行了测试，与PPO基线进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决均值-方差准则下最优做市问题的计算挑战，提出一种新的基于签名的方法替代传统强化学习方法。

Method: 采用签名线性化技术将做市问题转化为对增强市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化；开发了名为Sig-REINFORCE的签名算法来学习最优买卖报价。

Result: 在两种场景（泊松过程到达和自激霍克斯过程到达的市价单）下进行了测试，并以Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)作为基线进行了基准对比。

Conclusion: 提出的签名方法能有效解决均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题，Sig-REINFORCE算法能够学习到最优买卖报价。

Abstract: We propose a signature-based method to solve the optimal market-making problem under a mean-variance criterion. By exploiting signature linearization techniques, we reduce the market-making problem to a pseudo-linear optimization over the expected signature of an augmented market path, and we develop a signature algorithm named Sig-REINFORCE to learn the optimal bid and ask quotes. We test our method in two scenarios, in which market-order arrivals follow either a Poisson or a self-exciting Hawkes process, and we benchmark it against a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) baseline.

</details>


### [212] [Dynamic Core Allocation for Malleable Jobs with Unknown Speed-up Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19789)
*S. ~A. Bodas,J. ~L. Dorsman,M. Mandjes,L. Ravner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对多核计算系统中可塑作业的动态资源分配问题，提出了一种迭代学习与控制框架，通过估计未知加速比参数并求解MDP来最小化长期平均作业数（即平均响应时间），并证明了估计量的一致性和算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多核计算系统中，可塑作业（可动态调整并行度）的资源分配面临参数未知的加速比函数带来的不确定性，需要设计能够在线学习并优化核心分配的策略，以最小化系统的平均响应时间。

Method: 提出迭代学习-控制框架，包含两个交替步骤：(1) 基于状态相关的作业间离开时间构建最大似然估计器，在固定分配策略下证明其强一致性；(2) 使用当前参数估计值求解MDP更新分配策略。每类作业内部均分核心，类间容量分配由MDP求解得到。提出两种将估计与动态规划策略更新相结合的算法。

Result: 证明了最大似然估计量在固定分配策略下具有强一致性；通过数值实验展示了所提两种学习算法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提迭代学习-控制框架能够有效应对两类不同加速比函数的可塑作业资源分配问题，为在线学习与动态优化相结合的调度方法提供了理论保证和实验验证。

Abstract: We study dynamic resource allocation in a multicore computing system with a fixed number of processing cores and a stream of {\it malleable} jobs. Each job may adjust its level of parallelism during execution, allowing adaptive redistribution of resources across concurrently active jobs. Jobs belong to one of two observable classes, each characterized by a distinct speed-up function with unknown parameters. The objective is to learn a core-allocation policy that minimizes the long-run mean number of jobs in the system, equivalently the mean response time in steady state.
  \noindent To address this uncertainty, we develop an iterative learning-and-control framework. The system alternates between estimating the unknown speed-up parameters from observed job completions and solving the associated Markov decision process (MDP) to update the allocation policy. Within each job class, cores are shared equally among active jobs; the fraction of capacity assigned to each class is obtained from the MDP formulation of \cite{berg2017}, evaluated at the current parameter estimates. We construct a maximum likelihood estimator based on state-dependent inter-departure times and prove its strong consistency under a fixed allocation policy. We further propose two learning algorithms that combine this estimation step with dynamic programming-based policy updates, and illustrate their through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [213] [Semiglobal Input-Delay Tolerance Algorithm for Distributed Nonconvex Optimization of Networked Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19871)
*Jing-Zhe Xu,Zhi-Wei Liu,Ming-Feng Ge,Yan-Wu Wang,Dinxin He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究输入延迟和非线性网络系统下的分布式优化问题，提出了输入延迟容忍半全局收敛概念，并开发了半全局输入延迟容忍算法，在Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件下可扩展到非凸优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法未充分考虑输入延迟与非线性动力学的耦合效应，需要一种能同时处理输入延迟、非线性动力学和共识约束的分布式优化算法。

Method: 采用分层设计框架与输入-状态稳定性分析，提出SIDT算法；通过Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件放松严格凸性要求，拓展至非凸优化场景。

Result: SIDT算法在实际中实现了IDTSC，即对任意预设紧致初始集，存在可容许延迟界使得节点状态在共识约束下收敛到最优解。

Conclusion: 数值实验验证了该算法在带输入延迟的非线性网络系统中的有效性，为处理更一般的分布式优化问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of distributed optimization problems in networked nonlinear systems (NNSs) subject to input delays and consensus constraints. It introduces input-delay tolerant semiglobal convergence (IDTSC), meaning that for any prescribed compact initial set there exists an admissible delay bound under which the optimal solution is computed within consensus constraints and all node states converge to the solution. Building on a hierarchical design and input-to-state stability analysis, a new semiglobal input-delay tolerant (SIDT) algorithm is developed that practically achieves IDTSC for distributed optimization under the coupling between input delays and nonlinear dynamics. Further, by relaxing strict convexity requirements through the Polyak-Łojasiewicz condition, the SIDT algorithm broadens its applicability to nonconvex optimization. Finally, numerical experiments corroborate the theory on NNSs with input delays.

</details>


### [214] [A Regularized Nikaido-Isoda Function Approach to Multi-Leader-Follower Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20013)
*Atsushi Hori,Takayuki Okuno,Ellen H. Fukuda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多领导者-追随者博弈（MLFG）重构方法，利用正则化的Nikaido-Isoda函数将MLFG近似为单层可微的纳什均衡问题，无需追随者博弈的导数信息或凸性假设，适用于更广泛的MLFG类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLFG求解方法（如转化为EPEC或代入响应函数）存在可扩展性差、需要高阶导数等局限性，因此需要一种更具通用性和可扩展性的新型重构方法。

Method: 利用正则化的Nikaido-Isoda函数，引入惩罚参数，将MLFG近似为一个单层可微的纳什均衡问题。

Result: 新方法不需要追随者博弈的导数信息，也不要求每个追随者问题的凸性，能处理更广泛的MLFG；在全局子解析条件下，分析了原始MLFG均衡与所提重构之间的数学关系。

Conclusion: 提出了一种更具通用性和可扩展性的MLFG重构方法，拓展了可处理的博弈类别，并给出了相应的理论保证。

Abstract: A multi-leader--follower game (MLFG) is a hierarchical noncooperative game in which leaders compete at the upper level while taking into account the followers' best responses at the lower level. A typical approach to solving the MLFG reformulates it as an equilibrium problem with equilibrium constraints (EPECs) by replacing the lower-level game with its KKT conditions. Another approach, when each follower's response is unique, is to reformulate the MLFG as a Nash equilibrium problem by substituting these response functions into each leader's problem. However, both reformulations may lack scalability since higher-order derivatives may be required when solving the resulting problems.
  In this paper, we propose a new reformulation of the MLFG by exploiting a regularized Nikaido--Isoda function and approximating the MLFG by a single-level differentiable Nash equilibrium problem with a penalty parameter. The proposed reformulation neither requires derivative information on the followers' game nor assumes convexity of each follower's problem; hence, it can handle a broader class of MLFGs. Under global subanalyticity, we analyze the mathematical relationship between equilibria of the original MLFG and the proposed reformulation.

</details>


### [215] [Optimization with inequality constraints by the embedded gradient vector field method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20059)
*Petre Birtea,Ioan Casu,Dan Comanescu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入二次松弛变量，将带不等式约束的优化问题转化为光滑流形上的几何问题，利用嵌入梯度向量场方法推导出KKT条件与最优性条件的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将不等式约束优化问题纳入黎曼几何框架，避免经典拉格朗日乘子法，从几何角度重新理解和推导最优性条件。

Method: 引入二次松弛变量将可行集提升为扩展空间中的光滑子流形；分析约束流形的分层结构；利用嵌入梯度向量场形式推导拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式；在每个分层上计算受限Hessian矩阵得到二阶最优性条件。

Result: 得到了拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式；恢复了经典KKT一阶必要条件；给出了互补松弛条件的几何对应——拉格朗日乘子的完全符号条件；通过双冰激凌锥例子验证了理论。

Conclusion: 成功建立了不等式约束优化问题的几何框架，为理解和推导最优性条件提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for constrained optimization problems with inequality constraints through the introduction of quadratic slack variables. This formulation makes it possible to employ the language of Riemannian geometry and to solve the problem via the embedded gradient vector field method. We lift the feasible set to a smooth submanifold of an extended ambient space. The stratified structure of the resulting constraint manifold is analyzed in detail, yielding a natural partition according to which constraints are active. Using the embedded gradient vector field formalism, we derive explicit, determinantal formulas for the Lagrange multiplier functions directly from the geometry of the constraint manifold, recovering and re-framing the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker first-order necessary conditions without invoking the classical Lagrange multiplier method. Second-order optimality conditions are obtained by computing the restricted Hessian on each stratum, and a complete sign condition on the Lagrange multipliers is identified as the geometric counterpart of the classical complementary slackness condition. The theory is illustrated on the double ice-cream cone example, where the geometry of the problem determines the nature and number of local minima.

</details>


### [216] [Optimal Coarse Correlated Equilibria in Mean Field Games: Linear Programming and No-Regret Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20062)
*Luciano Campi,Federico Cannerozzi,Ioannis Tzouanas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间平均场博弈，引入了最优粗相关均衡的概念，建立了线性规划公式，证明了最优粗相关均衡的存在性，并设计了基于无遗憾原始-对偶算法的学习方法，同时给出了算法的收敛速率和数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均场博弈中，传统均衡概念（如纳什均衡）计算复杂且存在多重性问题。粗相关均衡提供了一种更灵活的均衡概念，且可以通过线性规划高效求解。本文旨在将这一框架推广到连续时间平均场博弈，并实现最优均衡的选择与学习。

Method: 首先形式化连续时间平均场博弈中的粗相关均衡概念；然后建立线性规划公式以刻画所有粗相关均衡；证明LP最优解的存在性并建立与原始概率设定之间的联系；最后基于外部遗憾约束的拉格朗日对偶形式，设计无遗憾的原始-对偶学习算法。

Result: 成功证明了连续时间平均场博弈中最优粗相关均衡的存在性；建立了完整的LP表征理论；所提出的原始-对偶算法具有明确的收敛速率保证；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为连续时间平均场博弈提供了一种可计算的最优粗相关均衡框架，兼具理论严谨性与算法实用性，拓展了平均场博弈中均衡选择与学习的边界。

Abstract: We introduce optimal coarse correlated equilibria for continuous-time mean field games. A coarse correlated equilibrium is a randomized recommendation scheme from which no player can gain by ignoring the recommendation and switching to an alternative strategy. The problem is as follows: a moderator selects, among all mean-field coarse correlated equilibria, one that optimizes a prescribed performance criterion, which may differ from the representative player's objective. After formulating the problem, we develop a linear programming (LP) formulation, prove the existence of optimal LP coarse correlated equilibria, and relate the LP characterization to the original probabilistic setting. Building on this characterization, we design a no-regret primal-dual algorithm, based on an equivalent Lagrangian formulation of the external-regret constraint, for learning such equilibria. We provide explicit convergence rates for the learning algorithm, and numerical examples illustrate the method.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Averaging in John Ellipsoid Approximation: High-Accuracy Algorithms in the Leverage-Score Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20082)
*Xiaoyu Li,Junwei Yu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对称多面体John椭球计算中，现有杠杆分数算法将复杂度$\varepsilon^{-1}$归因于精度需求实为认证伪象，通过将成本分解为认证、识别、精度三部分，提出预热加速方法可将精度依赖降为双对数级别，仅需$C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有杠杆分数算法（从Cohen等人2019年到最新成果）在计算对称多面体John椭球时均达到$(1+\varepsilon)$-近似需要$\Theta(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(n/d))$次迭代，但作者认为这一复杂度将三种不同成本混为一谈，希望分离出真正的瓶颈所在。

Method: 1. 将John椭球问题等价转化为D-最优设计形式$\min_{\mathbf{p}\in\Delta_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$，建立杠杆分数预言机与一阶预言机的对应关系；2. 将成本分解为认证（certification）、识别（identification）、精度（accuracy）三类；3. 证明均匀平均迭代作为认证证书的gap恰为$\Theta(1/T)$，揭示$\varepsilon^{-1}$来自认证而非精度；4. 提出预热加速方法：先进行$\varepsilon$无关的初始化$C(\mathbf{A})$，再用加速方法$O(\sqrt{\kappa}\log(1/\varepsilon))$步，识别最优面后用阻尼Newton法仅需$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$步。

Result: 精度依赖从$\varepsilon^{-1}$降为双对数级别$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$，总查询复杂度为$C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$，其中$C(\mathbf{A})$为$\varepsilon$无关的条件依赖初始化成本。开放问题在于识别成本（到达最优面的条件无关界）及下界。

Conclusion: 精度并非John椭球计算的障碍；$\varepsilon^{-1}$是认证伪象，而非真正的精度成本。通过更精细的成本分解和算法设计，可实现精度依赖的双对数复杂度。

Abstract: The John ellipsoid of a symmetric polytope $P=\{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^d:\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{x}\|_\infty\le1\}$, $\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, is computed by a long line of leverage-score algorithms, from Cohen, Cousins, Lee and Yang (COLT 2019) to its successors [WY24, CLS+25], all reaching a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation in $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(n/d))$ iterations. We separate this complexity into three costs the modern line conflates (certification, identification, and accuracy) and locate the historical $\varepsilon^{-1}$ in the first alone. In the equivalent D-optimal-design form $\min_{\mathbf{p}\inΔ_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$, the leverage-score oracle is exactly the first-order oracle and the $(1+\varepsilon)$-John guarantee the Frank-Wolfe gap $g(\mathbf{p})\le\varepsilon d$; through this dictionary the costs come apart. The $\varepsilon^{-1}$ is a certification artifact: the uniform average of the iterates, the certificate used throughout the line, has gap exactly $Θ(1/T)$, however cheap each iteration is made. Pointed instead at the last iterate the same oracle is fast: a warm-started accelerated method reaches the guarantee in $C(\mathbf{A})+O(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries after an $\varepsilon$-independent setup $C(\mathbf{A})$, and once the optimal face is identified the facial problem is an unconstrained self-concordant minimization whose Hessian the oracle recovers exactly, so damped Newton needs only $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ steps, for a total of $C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries. The accuracy dependence is thus doubly logarithmic after an $\varepsilon$-independent, condition-dependent setup; the open problem is the remaining identification cost (a condition-free bound on reaching the optimal face) and lower bounds. Accuracy is not the obstruction.

</details>


### [218] [Optimizing Agricultural Drone Operations: From Launch and Recovery Siting to Tiered Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20239)
*Ethan Kolby,Josh Noble,Max Z. Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个用于农业无人机喷洒作业的操作框架，通过对比设施选址方法和分层路径规划参数，实现了在分钟级时间内完成规划，且服务面积仅略有下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业中无人机应用日益增多，但现有优化工具随问题规模增大出现指数级运行时间，难以满足日常运营的高效规划需求。

Method: 采用混合整数规划（MIP）基线对比p-中位启发式方法进行设施选址；采用分层问题分解方法将目标区域划分为6-8个空间聚类进行路径规划。

Result: p-中位启发式方法将运行时间从超过97秒降至不到1.2秒（降低三个数量级），服务面积仅减少4%；分层路径规划将计算时间降低一个数量级，服务面积退化极小。

Conclusion: 该框架能在商用硬件上实现分钟级规划，具有实际运营可行性；未来工作将纳入天气建模、设施选址与路径规划的集成优化，并在不同田块几何形状上验证。

Abstract: Drones are increasingly used in agriculture, where tight margins demand efficient planning. Current optimization tools suffer from exponential runtimes as problem sizes grow, necessitating practical heuristics for daily operations. This paper presents an operational framework and benchmarking analysis for drone spraying operations. We evaluate the trade-offs between facility siting methods and tiered routing parameters. For facility siting, comparing a Mixed-Integer Program (MIP) baseline against a $p$-Median heuristic shows that the heuristic reduces runtime by three orders of magnitude, from over 97 seconds to under 1.2 seconds, with only a 4\% reduction in serviced field area. For route planning, a tiered problem decomposition approach partitioning the target area into 6 to 8 spatial clusters reduces computation time by an order of magnitude with minimal degradation in serviced area. This framework achieves minute-scale planning on commodity hardware, demonstrating operational relevance. Future research will incorporate weather modeling, integrated optimization of facility location and routing, and validation across diverse field geometries.

</details>


### [219] [Diagonal Hessian Approximation Based on Conjugacy Condition for Noisy Derivative-Free Optimization Problems in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20304)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Saman Babaie--Kafaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对大规模噪声导数优化问题，提出一种用对角近似替代仿射缩放矩阵的进化策略方法，在噪声环境下比传统MAES方法更高效稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模噪声导数优化问题中，矩阵自适应进化策略(MAES)在噪声水平较大时性能下降，因为排序和选择会错误识别信息性采样点，影响重组步骤和缩放信息的学习，尤其在髙维场景下效率显著降低。

Method: 提出一种无需梯度估计的DFO方法，用基于共轭类型条件的对角近似替代完整的仿射缩放矩阵，使用连续的归一化重组位移进行保守的对角更新，限制不可靠选择信息的影响。

Result: 在噪声基准问题上，所提方法与MAES基线相比具有竞争力，且在噪声水平较大、基于排名的选择变得不可靠时，通常更高效。

Conclusion: 提出的对角更新方法在计算上比全矩阵自适应和有限内存仿射缩放变体更便宜，同时在噪声环境中提供稳定的缩放机制。

Abstract: We consider large-scale noisy derivative-free optimization (DFO) problems in which only function values are available and gradient or subgradient information cannot be reliably estimated. Matrix-adaptation evolution strategies (MAES) and their limited-memory variants are among the most robust DFO methods under noise; however, their performance may deteriorate when the noise level is large. In such regimes, sorting and selection may misidentify informative sampled points, making the recombination step less reliable and weakening the scaling information used by affine or matrix-adaptation mechanisms. This can substantially reduce the efficiency of MAES-type methods, especially in high-dimensional settings.
  To address this limitation, we propose a DFO method that replaces the full affine-scaling matrix with a diagonal approximation constructed from conjugacy-type conditions. The proposed mechanism does not attempt to estimate gradients, subgradients, or interpolation models, nor does it learn dense covariance information from noisy rankings. Instead, it uses consecutive normalized recombination displacements in a conservative diagonal update, thereby limiting the influence of unreliable selection information while preserving the derivative-free structure of the underlying evolutionary framework. As a result, the method is computationally cheaper than full matrix-adaptation schemes and limited-memory affine-scaling variants, while providing a stable scaling mechanism in noisy environments. Numerical experiments on noisy benchmark problems show that the proposed method is competitive with, and often more efficient than, MAES-type baselines, particularly when the noise level is large and ranking-based selection becomes unreliable.

</details>


### [220] [Robust $Q$-learning for mean-field control under Wasserstein uncertainty in common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20356)
*Mathieu Laurière,Ariel Neufeld,Kyunghyun Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Wasserstein不确定性下离散时间平均场控制问题的鲁棒Q学习算法，该算法结合了量化投影方案与Wasserstein对偶重构，建立了同步和异步学习方案的收敛性与有限时间迭代界，并在系统风险和流行病模型上进行了数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在公共噪声律存在Wasserstein不确定性时，离散时间平均场控制问题的鲁棒学习算法缺失问题，特别是需要同时处理模型不确定性和高维公共噪声空间的挑战。

Method: 将量化投影方案与公共噪声空间上的Wasserstein对偶重构相结合，设计了同步和异步两种Q学习算法，并建立了相应的收敛性理论与有限时间迭代界。

Result: 在系统风险和流行病模型上的数值实验表明：异步实现与理想化Bellman迭代相比表现良好；展示了在公共噪声误指定情况下的鲁棒性-性能权衡；报告了异步Q学习算法的观测收敛行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒Q学习算法能有效处理Wasserstein不确定性下的平均场控制问题，具有理论收敛保证和良好的数值表现，为实际应用中的模型不确定性提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this article, we present a robust $Q$-learning algorithm for discrete-time mean-field control problems under Wasserstein uncertainty in the common noise law. The algorithm combines a quantization-and-projection scheme with a Wasserstein dual reformulation on the common-noise space. We establish its convergence together with finite-time iteration bounds for both synchronous and asynchronous learning schemes. Numerical experiments on systemic risk and epidemic models compare the asynchronous implementation with an idealized Bellman iteration, illustrate the robustness-performance tradeoff under common-noise misspecification, and report the observed convergence behavior of the asynchronous $Q$-learning algorithm.

</details>


### [221] [A Single-Loop Minorized Dual Decomposition Method for Nonsmooth Multi-Stage Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20383)
*Dan Luo,Hailin Sun,Lei Yang,Yang You*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有非光滑复合目标的多阶段随机规划问题，提出了一种单循环极小化对偶分解方法，该方法通过构建极小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，并采用非精确ADMM求解，实现了阶段和场景可分解的并行计算框架，验证了全局收敛性和良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机规划问题具有内在的阶段和场景结构，现有方法难以有效利用这种结构特性，且存在计算效率低、不适合并行实现等问题。

Method: 提出单循环极小化对偶分解方法：每轮迭代构建一个极小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，然后在该对偶问题上执行一次基于对称高斯-赛德尔迭代的非精确交替方向乘子法，以生成下一轮迭代点。

Result: 对于三阶段情形建立了全局收敛性，并将收敛定理推广到一般多阶段设定；数值实验验证了该框架的计算可行性和相对于阶段与场景结构良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够保持MSP问题的阶段和场景可分解结构，适合并行实现，具有理论收敛保证和良好的实际计算性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi-stage stochastic programming (MSP) problems with nonsmooth composite objectives. Tailored to their intrinsic stage-wise and scenario-wise structure, we develop a single-loop minorized dual decomposition method, in which each iteration constructs a minorized problem and its restricted Wolfe dual, and then performs \textit{one iteration} of the symmetric Gauss--Seidel based inexact alternating direction method of multipliers on the resulting dual problem to generate the next iterate. A key feature of the proposed optimization framework is that the resulting updates preserve the stage-wise and scenario-wise decomposable structure of the MSP problem and are suitable for parallel implementation. We establish global convergence of the generated iterates for the three-stage case and further establish the corresponding global convergence theorem for the general multi-stage setting. Numerical experiments illustrate the computational viability of the proposed framework and its favorable scaling behavior with respect to the stage-wise and scenario-wise structure.

</details>


### [222] [High-Probability Last-Iterate Guarantees for Two-Point Gaussian Zeroth-Order Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20446)
*Haishan Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑强凸随机优化问题，建立了标准同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法的高概率最后迭代保证，证明了当维度满足条件时，以至少1-δ的概率达到Õ(d/T)的收敛速率，且置信度依赖为对数级而非多项式级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶随机梯度方法的高概率分析通常依赖于马尔可夫不等式从期望界转换或对噪声进行截断，缺乏直接的高概率最后迭代保证。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立不依赖这些间接技术的直接分析方法。

Method: 采用同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法，每轮迭代抽取高斯方向，使用相同随机样本在两个对称扰动点处评估目标函数，并执行范数归一化的随机逼近步长。证明方法结合了高斯角度的均匀加权扫描与角度扩展的乘积鞅边界，用于控制由随机递归展开产生的带符号后缀乘积项。

Result: 在假设无偏随机梯度和随机梯度噪声平方范数满足条件指数矩边界的前提下，当d≥16log(6T/δ)时，f(x_T)-f(x*) = Õ(d/T) 以至少1-δ的概率成立。置信度依赖为对数级而非多项式级。

Conclusion: 本文首次在条件次高斯随机梯度噪声下，为同样本高斯递归零阶方法建立了直接的高概率最后迭代结果，其分析方法不依赖于期望界转换或噪声截断，为零阶优化的高概率分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: We establish a direct high-probability last-iterate guarantee for the standard same-sample two-point Gaussian zeroth-order stochastic-gradient method applied to smooth, strongly convex stochastic optimization. At each iteration, the method draws a fresh Gaussian direction, evaluates the objective at two symmetric perturbations using the same stochastic sample, and takes a norm-normalized stochastic-approximation step. Assuming unbiased stochastic gradients and a conditional exponential-moment bound on the squared norm of the stochastic-gradient noise, we prove that, whenever \(d\ge16\log(6T/δ)\), \[ f(\bx_T)-f(\bx^*) =
\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(\frac{d}{T}\right) \] with probability at least \(1-δ\), up to fixed problem parameters and logarithmic factors. The confidence dependence is therefore logarithmic rather than polynomial in \(1/δ\). The analysis is direct: it neither invokes Markov's inequality to convert an expectation bound nor truncates the noise. We are not aware of a prior direct high-probability last-iterate result at this zeroth-order scale for the same-sample Gaussian recursion under conditional sub-Gaussian stochastic-gradient noise. The proof combines a uniform weighted scan for Gaussian angles with an angle-enlarged product-martingale boundary that controls the signed suffix-product term arising from the unrolled stochastic recursion.

</details>


### [223] [CLUSTER: Derivative-free optimization of smooth functions with parameter-change costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20498)
*Serena Landers,Sahil Pontula,Shiekh Zia Uddin,Sachin Vaidya,Marin Soljačić,Steven G. Johnson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出CLUSTER算法，一种针对参数变更存在成本（如机器人实验室实验）的局部无导数优化算法。该算法基于Powell和Conn的二次插值优化方法，在二阶可导目标函数上性能提升约50%，显著优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead算法，并具有收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在诸如机器人控制实验室实验等场景中，调整每个参数（或参数簇）存在独立的成本（如机器人每次移动的成本），现有无导数优化算法未考虑此类参数变更成本，需要一种能有效处理该成本模型的优化算法。

Method: CLUSTER算法（坐标级更新策略的信任域步长评估改进）：基于Powell和Conn的二次插值优化算法框架，引入坐标级别的参数更新策略，在信任域步骤中评估并优化参数簇的变更成本。

Result: 在多种测试问题（包括光学实验室实验）上，CLUSTER变体相比基线算法性能提升约50%；显著优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead等常见实验室优化算法；通过对Conn算法的收敛性证明进行改编，获得了CLUSTER-Conn的收敛性保证。

Conclusion: CLUSTER算法有效解决了存在参数变更成本的无导数优化问题，在实验室场景中表现优异，兼具理论收敛保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: We introduce the CLUSTER algorithm (\textbf{c}oordinate-\textbf{l}evel \textbf{u}pdate \textbf{s}trategy for \textbf{t}rust-region step \textbf{e}valuation \textbf{r}efinement) for local derivative-free optimization problems where there is a cost to changing each parameter (or clusters of parameters). For example, this type of cost model is appropriate for optimizing robot-controlled laboratory experiments, in which a robot may incur a separate motion for each parameter cluster to be adjusted. We build off of a class of quadratic-interpolation optimization algorithms by Powell and Conn that are known to perform well for twice-differentiable objectives (e.g. low-noise experiments), and show that the CLUSTER variants improve performance on a variety of test problems (including an optics laboratory experiment) by around 50$\%$, and greatly outperform common competing algorithms for laboratory optimization (Bayesian optimization and Nelder--Mead). We also adapt the convergence proof of the Conn algorithm to obtain a similar convergence guarantee for CLUSTER-Conn.

</details>


### [224] [On Second-Order Methods for Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20534)
*Jiawen Bi,Jiaxiang Li,Mingyi Hong,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双层优化中二阶稳定点（SOSP）的高效求解问题，首次提出了一个确定性单循环三次正则牛顿算法，在下层强凸的约束下实现了最优的O(ε^{-1.5})总 oracle 复杂度，并证明了该收敛率是理论最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双层优化中寻找二阶稳定点（SOSP）的理论与算法尚不完善。即使下层问题强凸，诱导的超函数通常非凸。尽管三次正则牛顿法（CRN）在单层优化中达到了最优的O(ε^{-1.5}) SOSP 率，但如何控制双层优化中应用二阶方法时超梯度和超Hessian的计算精度以保证整体效率，这一问题尚未解决。

Method: 1) 提出双循环CRN基线方法，实现最优外层率但需重复求解下层问题。2) 提出单循环三次正则牛顿算法，将一步下层梯度更新与一步超梯度牛顿更新相结合。3) 证明该方法在确定性的非凸上层-强凸下层（NCSC）双层优化中，实现了O(ε^{-1.5})的总 oracle 复杂度。

Result: 所提出的单循环算法达到了O(ε^{-1.5})的最优总 oracle 复杂度，且这是首次在NCSC双层优化中实现该最优率的确定性单循环方法。

Conclusion: 本文为双层优化中二阶稳定点的高效求解提供了理论最优的算法框架，填补了该领域在理论收敛率与算法设计方面的空白。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is an indispensable modeling tool for modern machine learning and engineering design. However, the theory and practice for finding second order stationary points in the context of bilevel optimization still remain largely unsettled. Even for bilevel optimization with strongly convex lower-level problem, the hyperfunction it induces is in general nonconvex. Although the Cubic Regularized Newton methods (CRN) famously achieve the optimal $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ SOSP (second-order stationary point) rate in single-level optimization, it is unclear how to control the accuracy of the hypergradient and hyper-Hessian computations in the context of applying the second-order methods to bilevel problems in order for the overall process to be efficient. In this paper, we set out to answer this question. In particular, we first formulate a double loop CRN baseline that achieves the optimal outer rate but requires repeated lower level solves. Next, we propose a single loop cubic regularized Newton algorithm that combines one lower-level gradient step with one Newton step for the hypergradient, and prove an overall deterministic $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ total oracle complexity, which is optimal. In addition, we illustrate that some intuitively simple modifications of our method may fail to hold up the convergence result. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first deterministic single loop method for unconstrained NCSC (non-convex upper-level and strongly convex lower-level) bilevel optimization setting that achieves the $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ optimal convergence rate for finding an $\varepsilon$-SOSP of the hyperfunction.

</details>
